So... yes.\n\nDoctor: Do you find yourself starting tasks at home and then switching to something else before finishing, or having trouble staying focused on one thing even when you’re not at work?\n\nPatient: Um... I'm not really sure. I haven't really noticed it at home, I guess. I mostly just... keep to myself when I'm there. It's really just at the shop where it's been an issue.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any difficulty focusing or feeling restless in situations outside of work, like when you're relaxing at home or spending time with others?\n\nPatient: Um... no, not really.
